# Per-file obligation audit — `src/evp/digest.rs`

> **Status: this is a file-level obligation audit, not `SAFETY_DISCHARGED`.** It enumerates and attributes the file's production unsafe sites; it does not establish whole-file safety, it does not discharge the residual obligations listed below, and it does not constitute an `INDEPENDENTLY_CHECKED` result. A file graduates only through the AGENTS.md §7 dependency-closure and independent-evidence gates.

## 1. Reusable template

| Field | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `file` | Project-relative source path. |
| `candidate SHA` | The source identity the census was generated from, plus the audit HEAD if they differ. |
| `census counts` | Total operations, production operations, test-only operations, and the file hash from `atlas-refresh/unsafe-census.json`. |
| `group id` | Stable per-file identifier, `D001`-`Dnnn`. |
| `lines` | Every census line in the group, with the group count. |
| `kind` | Census kind code: `UF`, `UB`, `FC`, `HR`, `EC`, `UI`. |
| `ABI/export class` | `safe fn`, `unsafe fn`, or `unsafe extern "C"`, with `exported` or `not exported`. Only an exported FFI entry can carry a caller/ABI precondition. |
| `operation` | The actual raw/FFI operation(s) at those lines. |
| `contract` | The validity contract relied on: SAFETY comment, owner/slot type, FFI ABI, or `CtxDestroy` contract. |
| `establishment` | Who establishes the precondition: this code, an in-file caller, or an external C/ABI/provider contract. |
| `dependency group` | `D###` ids of groups whose obligations this row inherits, or `—` if none. |
| `state` | `CONTRACTED` (contract stated, not established), `ESTABLISHED` (precondition established by code or an identified caller/ABI contract), or `UNATTRIBUTED` (no caller-side establishment or boundary attribution). |
| `evidence needed` | Exactly what would be required before the group can become `INDEPENDENTLY_CHECKED`. |
| `residuals` | Obligations that remain after the group's current contract, including callback reentrancy, raw member pointers, and caller-asserted lengths. |

The state vocabulary maps onto the AGENTS.md §21 lifecycle as `UNKNOWN -> CONTRACTED -> ESTABLISHED -> INDEPENDENTLY_CHECKED -> DISCHARGED`; this audit never claims the last two states.

### ABI/export class summary

| ABI/export class | Groups |
|---|---:|
| `unsafe extern "C" — exported` | 178 |
| `unsafe fn — not exported` | 56 |
| `unsafe extern "C" — not exported` | 16 |
| `safe fn (extern "C") — exported` | 2 |
| `n/a — unsafe impl` | 1 |
| `safe fn — not exported` | 1 |
| **Total** | **254** |

All 254 groups carry an ABI/export class. The 16 `unsafe extern "C" — not exported` groups are private callbacks (fetch/OBJ table/null-digest visitors), so their establishment is in-file rather than an exported C ABI. The two `evp_md_up_ref`/`evp_md_free` rows were restated from an external-C-caller basis to the in-file `evp_generic_fetch` callback contract; no ESTABLISHED row now relies on an ABI that its export status does not support.

## 5. Residual obligation register

These obligations remain after the current contracts. They are not repaired by a SAFETY comment.

1. **Provider/application callback reentrancy** — every `EvpMd` function pointer (`newctx`, `dinit`, `dupdate`, `dfinal`, `dsqueeze`, `digest`, `freectx`, `copyctx`, `dupctx`, `get_params`, `set_ctx_params`, `get_ctx_params`, `gettable_params`, `settable_ctx_params`, `gettable_ctx_params`, `cleanup`, `init`, `update`, `final_`, `copy`, `md_ctrl`) and the signature callbacks in `do_sigver_init` are caller/provider-controlled. The code often reads context or method fields after the callback returns; a callback that frees or mutates the object is not guarded. Affected groups include the rows for `evp_md_from_algorithm`, `cleanup_old_md_data`, `evp_md_ctx_clear_digest`, `evp_md_ctx_free_algctx`, `evp_md_init_internal`, `evp_md_init_legacy`, `evp_md_final_legacy`, all `EVP_Digest*` provider paths, and the `do_sigver_init`/`sigver_*` family.
2. **Raw owning member pointers not mediated by a slot** — `EvpMd::type_name` (owned `*mut c_char`), `EvpMd::prov` (raw provider reference), `EvpMdCtx::md_data` (owned legacy block), `EvpMdCtx::fetched_digest` (owned method reference), `EvpMdCtx::pctx` (owned key context), `EvpMdCtx::algctx` (provider-owned opaque context), and the borrowed `digest`/`reqdigest` pointers. `cleanup_old_md_data`, `evp_md_ctx_clear_digest`, `EVP_MD_CTX_copy_ex`, `evp_md_ctx_copy_legacy`, and the destructor bodies reconstruct ownership by hand.
3. **Caller-asserted buffer extents and lengths** — `EVP_DigestUpdate`/`SignUpdate`/`VerifyUpdate` (`data`/`count`), `EVP_DigestFinal*`/`Squeeze` (`md`/`size`), `EVP_Digest`/`EVP_Q_digest`, `EVP_DigestSign*`/`EVP_DigestVerify*` (`tbs`, `sig`, `siglen`), `EVP_MD_CTX_ctrl` (`p1`/`p2`, including the `9999` fallback and negative `p1` cast to `usize`), and every `OSSL_PARAM` array. No group checks these extents; `RES_BUF` marks them.
4. **Aliasing and ownership in the copy paths** — **resolved except the legacy-fault divergence.** `EVP_MD_CTX_copy_ex` keeps the authority's decision order for a same-context copy: an uninitialised source resets; a provider source without `dupctx` returns 0 at `digest.c:615-617` before any copy; a provider source with `dupctx` reproduces the `copyctx` call, pcontext release, `md_data` cleanup and flag/update copy, or the `keep_fetched` reset when there is no `copyctx`. The legacy arm's self-copy is a NULL-method fault in the authority (`digest.c:684-720`), so it answers 1 without side effects instead (explicit §17 divergence; no authority result exists to reproduce). `evp_md_ctx_copy_legacy` takes its own reference on `fetched_digest` and duplicates `algctx` (or leaves it NULL) instead of aliasing the source's ownership; the allocation-failure path returns 0 without a reset, as the authority does. `EVP_MD_meth_dup` clears the non-owned-in-practice `type_name`; the authority's only assignment to it is in `evp_md_from_algorithm` (`digest.c:1040`), whose methods have `prov != NULL` and are refused by `EVP_MD_meth_dup` (`evp_lib.c:848`).
5. **Type-erased table data** — `set_legacy_nid` and `do_all_md_fn` cast `OBJ_NAME.data` to `EvpMd` based on the table's type argument; the data word is not type-tagged in this module.
6. **Static mutation** — `EVP_md_null()` returns the address of the immutable `N_MD`; `EVP_MD_meth_set_*` and `EVP_MD_up_ref`/`EVP_MD_free` do not reject `EVP_ORIG_GLOBAL` before some writes/reads. A C caller that casts away `const` can reach a write to static storage. The `StaticMd` `Sync` claim is a caller obligation, not a type-enforced one.
7. **Destructor implementer obligations** — `CtxDestroy::destroy_body` for `EvpMd` and `EvpMdCtx` is invoked by a safe `Drop`. The contract that it releases each member exactly once, never the enclosing block, and is valid for the zeroed state is asserted by the impl, not checked by the owner.
8. **Refcount/lifetime caller obligations** — `EVP_MD_up_ref`/`EVP_MD_free` assume a live object and a correct reference count; `EVP_MD_CTX_free` assumes allocator provenance and no double-free; `EVP_MD_CTX_get1_md` returns a legacy static as an owned pointer without a real reference.

## 6. Operations not classified

All 254 groups are assigned a row. Three of the five groups formerly left `UNATTRIBUTED` are now `CONTRACTED` through code changes: the raw-copy groups of `EVP_MD_CTX_copy_ex` and `evp_md_ctx_copy_legacy` (authority decision order preserved, self-copy handled explicitly, `fetched_digest`/`algctx` re-owned) and the shallow-copy group of `EVP_MD_meth_dup` (duplicate `type_name` cleared). Separately, and with no code change, the type-erased OBJ table cast in `set_legacy_nid` is now `CONTRACTED`: the cast is the authority's registration contract (`names.c:41`/`o_names.c:207`) and the NID read does not escape. The top-level `unsafe impl` group (`Sync for StaticMd`, two `CtxDestroy` impls) remains `UNATTRIBUTED`: its claims are unchanged and hold by construction (the one `StaticMd` is a private immutable static; the destroy bodies are the authority's own teardowns), but no independent Miri/negative-control harness has been run, so it stays a retained obligation.

## 8. What would make this audit `INDEPENDENTLY_CHECKED`

For each group, an independent reviewer would need: the exact source and build identity; a contract witness for each precondition (a caller, an ABI declaration, or an owner invariant); a runtime/static check that fires on a deliberate violation (Miri/tree-borrows, ASan/guard pages, custom allocator, callback-reentrancy harness, refcount/double-free negative control); and a written disposition of every residual. Absent those, the group remains `CONTRACTED` or `ESTABLISHED`, and the file remains not `SAFETY_DISCHARGED`.

## 4. Residual obligation register

These obligations remain after the current contracts. They are not repaired by a `SAFETY:` comment.

1. **Raw owning members and union arms** — `EvpPkeyCtx::propquery` (owned `*mut c_char`), `keymgmt`/`op_kex_exchange`/`op_sig_signature`/`op_ciph_cipher`/`op_encap_kem` (owned method references), `pkey`/`peerkey` (owned key references), `rsa_pubexp` (owned `BIGNUM`), and the cached `dist_id`/`dist_id_name` pair. `destroy_body` frees them directly (`src/evp/pkey_ctx.rs:1006-1020`), `evp_pkey_ctx_free_cached_data` blind-frees the cached pair (`:870-871`), and `evp_pkey_ctx_free_old_ops` selects one union arm from `operation` (`:766-805`). There is no owner slot or typed borrow that enforces these relations.
2. **Provider/table callback reentrancy** — `gettable_ctx_params`, `settable_ctx_params`, `set_ctx_params`, `get_ctx_params`, `gen_set_params`, `gen_get_params`, all four `freectx` and all four `dupctx` function pointers, and every `fixup_args` table row are caller/provider-controlled. The code commonly reads context fields or the method after the callback returns; a callback that frees or mutates the object is not guarded. The translation loop's `cleanup_translation_ctx` and the per-call `XlatCtx` are stack-owned, but the provider may re-enter the context.
3. **Caller-asserted lengths and pointer types** — `EVP_PKEY_CTX_set1_*`/`add1_*` and `set_mac_key` (`data`/`datalen`), `EVP_PKEY_CTX_get_group_name` (`name`/`namelen`), `EVP_PKEY_CTX_set_signature` (`sig`/`siglen`), `EVP_PKEY_CTX_set1_id` (`id`/`len`), `EVP_PKEY_CTX_ctrl`/`ctrl_str` (`p1`/`p2`), every `OSSL_PARAM` array, and all `fix_*` `p1`/`p2` pairs. No group checks these extents; the table row is the only type evidence.
4. **Hidden obligations in exported signatures** — `EVP_PKEY_CTX_get_signature_md` documents `md` as NULL-or-writable but writes `*md` unconditionally at `:9550` (and through the legacy GET_MD fixer); `EVP_PKEY_CTX_get1_id_len` documents `id_len` as NULL-or-writable but `fix_distid_len` writes `*(size_t *)p2` at `:3565`; `EVP_PKEY_meth_copy` documents only live pointers but `ptr::copy_nonoverlapping` at `:2145` is UB for the legal C self-copy `dst == src`. These are not discharged by the comments.
5. **Refcount and partial-failure ownership in `EVP_PKEY_CTX_dup`** — the new context stores `op_kex_exchange`/`op_sig_signature`/`op_ciph_cipher`/`op_encap_kem` before calling `up_ref` (`:1101`, `:1131`, `:1158`, `:1186`). If `up_ref` returns 0, the refusal path drops `rctx`, whose `destroy_body` releases the stored method pointer even though the new context never took a reference. The provider `dupctx` is also trusted to return a new algctx rather than the source pointer.
6. **Unchecked arithmetic on provider-controlled sizes** — `evp_pkey_ctx_add1_octet_string` computes `os_params[0].return_size + datalen as usize` at `:9835` and `info_len + datalen` at `:9876` without `checked_add`; `return_size` is provider-controlled. `evp_pkey_ctx_store_cached_data` receives `p1 as usize` from `EVP_PKEY_CTX_ctrl` at `:10551`, so a negative ctrl length becomes a huge `usize` before `CRYPTO_memdup` at `:10802`.
7. **Global registry state and borrowed methods** — `APP_PKEY_METHODS` is a `static mut` accessed without a lock by `EVP_PKEY_meth_add0` (`:2190-2209`), `EVP_PKEY_meth_remove` (`:2272`) and `evp_app_cleanup_int` (`:2249-2254`). The registry stores borrowed `*const EvpPkeyMethod` values with no lifetime tie; `EVP_PKEY_meth_free` only frees `DYNAMIC` methods, so freeing or re-registering the same method is a caller error.
8. **Borrowed strings and algorithm pointers** — `EvpPkeyCtx::keytype` is copied by `EVP_PKEY_CTX_dup` (`:1072`) and read by `fix_group_ecx` (`:6902`); `get_cipher_name`/`get_md_name` return borrowed names that `fix_cipher_md` passes to `strlen` without a NULL check (`:3465`, `:3480`); `EVP_PKEY_CTX_get_signature_md` trusts the provider to NUL-terminate the 80-byte name buffer before `evp_get_digestbyname_ex` (`:9544`).
9. **Destructor implementer obligations** — `CtxDestroy::destroy_body` for `EvpPkeyCtx` and `EvpPkeyMethod` is invoked by a safe `Drop`. The contract that each body releases each member exactly once, never the enclosing block, and is valid for the zeroed state is asserted by the impl, not checked by `OwnedCtx`. `evp_pkey_ctx_free_old_ops` does not clear `op_keymgmt_genctx` after `gen_cleanup` (`:802-804`); callers currently reset `operation` to `UNDEFINED`, which is what keeps the destructor from a second `gen_cleanup`, but the field itself is left dangling.
10. **Type-erased translation state** — `XlatCtx::p1`/`p2` and `XlatEntry` are a C-style tagged union without a Rust tag: `default_fixup_args` casts `p2` to `BigNum` (`:4281`) or to `char *` (`:4318-4329`) according to the table row, and every `fix_*` function does the same. The table row, not the type system, is the only guarantee.

## 5. Operations not classified

All 462 groups are assigned a row. No production site in `src/evp/pkey_ctx.rs` is left unclassified. The seven `UNATTRIBUTED` groups are the top-level unsafe-impl group, both `EVP_PKEY_meth_copy` groups, both `EVP_PKEY_CTX_get_signature_md` groups, and both `EVP_PKEY_CTX_get1_id_len` groups. The remaining groups are `CONTRACTED` or `ESTABLISHED` as recorded above; `CONTRACTED` is not a safety result.

## 6. Highest-risk groups

Only four mechanisms remain `UNATTRIBUTED`; therefore the fifth row below is the highest-risk `CONTRACTED` mechanism, included because the brief asks for five. It is labelled explicitly.

| Rank | Group | Group IDs | File:line | Why |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | unsafe impls (`Sync for XlatEntry`, two `CtxDestroy`) | D001 | src/evp/pkey_ctx.rs:990; :2095; :3179 | `UNATTRIBUTED`: `Sync` is not enforced against C mutation and both destructor bodies are implementer assertions called by a safe `Drop`; no Miri/negative-control harness. |
| 2 | `EVP_PKEY_meth_copy` | D116; D117 | src/evp/pkey_ctx.rs:2140; :2145 | `UNATTRIBUTED`: the C API permits `dst == src` (`*dst = *src`), but `ptr::copy_nonoverlapping` is UB for equal pointers; no distinctness check. |
| 3 | `EVP_PKEY_CTX_get_signature_md` | D042; D043 | src/evp/pkey_ctx.rs:9499; :9550 | `UNATTRIBUTED`: the `# Safety` contract allows a NULL `md`, but the provider arm writes `*md` unconditionally and the legacy GET_MD fixer writes through the same pointer; a NULL out-pointer is a hidden obligation. |
| 4 | `EVP_PKEY_CTX_get1_id_len` | D028; D029 | src/evp/pkey_ctx.rs:10442; :3565 | `UNATTRIBUTED`: the `# Safety` contract allows a NULL `id_len`, but `fix_distid_len` writes `*(size_t *)p2` without a NULL check. |
| 5 | `EVP_PKEY_CTX_dup` | D012; D013 | src/evp/pkey_ctx.rs:1045; :1101 | `CONTRACTED` (highest-risk non-UNATTRIBUTED): method pointers are stored before `up_ref`, so a failed `up_ref` makes `Drop` release the source's reference; provider `dupctx` is trusted not to alias the source algctx. |

## 7. What would make this audit `INDEPENDENTLY_CHECKED`

For each group, an independent reviewer would need: the exact source and build identity; a contract witness for every precondition (a caller, an ABI declaration, a table row, or an owner invariant); a runtime/static check that fires on a deliberate violation (Miri/tree-borrows, ASan/guard pages, custom allocator, callback-reentrancy harness, refcount/double-free negative control); and a written disposition of every residual. For this file the minimum additional controls are a self-copy control for `EVP_PKEY_meth_copy`, NULL out-parameter controls for `get_signature_md` and `get1_id_len`, a failed-`up_ref` control for `EVP_PKEY_CTX_dup`, an overflowing provider `return_size` control for `add1_octet_string`, and a negative-length ctrl control for the cached-data path. Absent those, every group remains `CONTRACTED` or `ESTABLISHED`, and the file remains not `SAFETY_DISCHARGED`.
